## 2. Purpose and Acceptance Boundary

Contract 7 owns the complete semantic-case and corpus-specification
architecture for Candidate A:

```text
SemanticCase identity;
Residential-34 × scenario-family cell identity;
ordinary and special primary-bucket assignment;
secondary diagnostic labels;
development/held-out allocation;
lineage and leakage prevention;
unique RoomCase counting;
sufficiency;
completeness;
expected-family assignment;
adjudication;
exclusion and replacement;
case lifecycle and sealing;
Contract-7 rules, validations, failures, escalations and EN/RU
localization.
```

Contract 7 defines normative annotation semantics. It does not create,
collect, populate or seal a corpus and does not authorize annotation
execution.

Acceptance of this Contract, if later granted, would still not
authorize:

```text
Candidate Lock;
repository persistence;
corpus or image acquisition;
annotation execution;
fixture creation;
provider/model invocation;
implementation;
Contract 8 or Contracts 8–10;
Controlled Learning activation;
Diagnosability Architecture;
Security Architecture.
```

## 6. Runtime and Data Boundary

```text
Operation
→ RoomCase[exactly 1]
→ ImageAsset[1..6]
→ one consolidated PerceptionResult
```

The Contract-7 counting and annotation unit is one RoomCase.

One ImageAsset is not one SemanticCase. Multiple RoomCases are never
merged into one runtime result.

In scope:

```text
one physical and materially unchanged room;
same-room validation;
cross-view matching;
contradiction preservation;
evidence fusion inside one RoomCase;
licensed, synthetic and staged sources.
```

Out of scope:

```text
multi-room runtime;
whole-home graph;
floor plans;
video;
panorama;
2.5D/3D reconstruction;
cross-session fusion;
commercial property;
real user photographs.
```

## 9. Scenario-Family Registry

| Stable ID | Token | EN label | RU label | EN predicate | RU predicate |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| c7.scenariofamily.001 | normal-readable | Normal readable | Обычная, легко читаемая сцена | RoomCase is clearly legible and none of the other five scenario predicates dominates. | RoomCase отчётливо читаем; ни один из пяти остальных сценарных предикатов не является доминирующим. |
| c7.scenariofamily.002 | moderate-clutter | Moderate clutter | Умеренная загромождённость | At least three additional visible Objects distributed across at least two support surfaces or floor zones are present beyond category-essential objects, while sufficiency remains resolvable. | Помимо объектов, необходимых для идентификации категории, видны не менее трёх дополнительных объектов минимум на двух опорных поверхностях или участках пола; достаточность остаётся разрешимой. |
| c7.scenariofamily.003 | partial-occlusion | Partial occlusion | Частичное загораживание | An identifiable foreground object obscures at least half of an annotatable target or hides a category-, relation-, sufficiency- or completeness-critical feature; ambiguous cases require adjudication. | Идентифицируемый объект переднего плана закрывает не менее половины аннотируемой цели либо скрывает признак, критичный для категории, отношения, достаточности или полноты; неоднозначные случаи требуют разрешения. |
| c7.scenariofamily.004 | partial-room-framing | Partial-room framing | Частичный кадр комнаты | The frame excludes a non-trivial portion of the room, but no valid continuation marker is sealed; therefore the case can remain ordinary only with completeness=full. | Кадр исключает нетривиальную часть комнаты, но валидный маркер продолжения не запечатан; поэтому случай может оставаться обычным только при completeness=full. |
| c7.scenariofamily.005 | lighting-variation | Lighting variation | Вариация освещения | Visible lighting materially alters scene legibility or appearance without making the RoomCase insufficient; examples include strong backlight, low ambient light, colored light or high contrast. | Видимое освещение существенно меняет читаемость или внешний вид сцены, но не делает RoomCase недостаточным; примеры: сильный контровой свет, слабое общее освещение, цветной свет или высокий контраст. |
| c7.scenariofamily.006 | camera-angle-variation | Camera-angle variation | Вариация ракурса камеры | The visible viewpoint is materially high, low or oblique and changes visible evidence compared with a typical staging view, while sufficiency remains resolvable. | Видимая точка съёмки существенно высокая, низкая или косая и меняет доступные свидетельства относительно типового постановочного ракурса, при этом достаточность остаётся разрешимой. |

### 9.1 Closed scenario compatibility matrix

| Scenario | Sufficiency | Completeness | Primary eligibility | Secondary eligibility | Ambiguity handling |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| normal-readable | must be sufficient | full only | allowed | allowed when independently true | adjudication if another primary predicate also matches |
| moderate-clutter | must be sufficient | full only | allowed | allowed when independently true | apply c7.qualifiercriterion.006; adjudicate uncertainty |
| partial-occlusion | must be sufficient | full only | allowed | allowed when independently true | apply c7.qualifiercriterion.003; adjudicate uncertainty |
| partial-room-framing | must be sufficient | full only; no valid marker | allowed | allowed when independently true | marker-positive case is not ordinary |
| lighting-variation | must be sufficient | full only | allowed | allowed when independently true | insufficient case is special, not ordinary |
| camera-angle-variation | must be sufficient | full only | allowed | allowed when independently true | apply c7.qualifiercriterion.007; adjudicate uncertainty |

No ordinary scenario family may carry sealed completeness=partial.


## 10. Primary and Secondary Label Architecture

```text
Ordinary case:
exactly one c7.ordinarycell.* primary bucket.

Special case:
exactly one c7.specialgroup.* primary bucket.

Mutual exclusion:
never both.

Secondary labels:
0..N scenario-family identities;
ordinary cases only;
diagnostic only;
never count toward minima;
never change subset, expected family or completeness.
```

No source establishes a deterministic ranking among multiple matching
ordinary scenarios. Therefore:

```text
multiple ordinary predicates true
→ c7.adjudicationtrigger.001
→ affirm one source-supported primary assignment,
   affirm one alternative source-supported assignment,
   exclude-and-replace,
   or fail-to-adjudicate.
```

No guessed precedence is permitted.

## 12. Special-Group Registry

| Stable ID | Token | EN label | RU label | EN definition | RU definition |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| c7.specialgroup.001 | low-information | Low-information | Малоинформативный | The case is not empty/near-empty; it contains no more than two independent design-relevant evidence units and may be sufficient or insufficient. | Случай не является пустым/почти пустым; он содержит не более двух независимых значимых единиц свидетельства и может быть достаточным или недостаточным. |
| c7.specialgroup.002 | empty-or-near-empty | Empty or near-empty | Пустая или почти пустая комната | The room contains zero design-relevant Objects or one incidental Object that does not contribute to category, sufficiency, scenario or completeness assignment. | Комната содержит ноль значимых объектов либо один случайный объект, не влияющий на категорию, достаточность, сценарий или полноту. |
| c7.specialgroup.003 | meaningful-partial-scene | Meaningful partial-scene | Значимая частичная сцена | The case is sufficient and at least one valid continuation marker is sealed; expected family is SceneResult and completeness is partial. | Случай достаточен, и запечатан минимум один валидный маркер продолжения; ожидаемое семейство — SceneResult, полнота — partial. |
| c7.specialgroup.004 | genuine-insufficient-evidence | Genuine insufficient-evidence | Подлинно недостаточные свидетельства | The case is insufficient and satisfies neither empty/near-empty nor low-information content-shape predicates after adjudication. | Случай недостаточен и после разрешения неоднозначности не удовлетворяет предикатам пустой/почти пустой или малоинформативной сцены. |

### 12.1 Fixed minima

| Special group | Development | Held-out |
|---|---|---|
| c7.specialgroup.001 low-information | 3 | 6 |
| c7.specialgroup.002 empty-or-near-empty | 2 | 5 |
| c7.specialgroup.003 meaningful-partial-scene | 5 | 10 |
| c7.specialgroup.004 genuine-insufficient-evidence | 5 | 10 |

### 12.2 Closed primary-bucket decision table

Apply rows in order. The first satisfied row is the only valid primary
bucket.

| Priority | Condition | Primary bucket | Expected family | Completeness |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | sufficiency=sufficient AND ≥1 valid continuation marker | meaningful-partial-scene | SceneResult | partial |
| 2 | empty-or-near-empty predicate true; row 1 false | empty-or-near-empty | SceneResult if sufficient; otherwise InsufficientEvidenceResult | full if sufficient and resolvable; no SceneResult completeness value when insufficient |
| 3 | low-information predicate true; rows 1–2 false | low-information | SceneResult if sufficient; otherwise InsufficientEvidenceResult | full if sufficient and resolvable; no SceneResult completeness value when insufficient |
| 4 | sufficiency=insufficient; rows 1–3 false | genuine-insufficient-evidence | InsufficientEvidenceResult | no SceneResult completeness value |
| 5 | sufficiency=sufficient; no marker; rows 1–4 false | one ordinary cell | SceneResult | full |
| 6 | any predicate ambiguity prevents one row | none yet | none yet | unresolved; adjudication required |

This table removes overlap among all four special groups and between
ordinary and special buckets.


## 13. Ordinary/Special Mutual Exclusivity

Primary-count invariant:

```text
one active SemanticCase
→ exactly one primary bucket
→ exactly one minimum-count contribution
```

Invalid combinations:

| Combination | Disposition |
|---|---|
| one ordinary cell + any special group | invalid |
| two ordinary cells | adjudication required before sealing |
| two special groups | apply §12.2; if still ambiguous, adjudication required |
| secondary label counted as primary | invalid |
| one RoomCase counted once per ImageAsset | invalid |
| ineligible derivative counted separately | invalid |
| excluded/superseded/invalidated case counted | invalid |

Population counts are computed only from sealed active case revisions.

| imageAssetMembershipSet[1..6] | R | R | R | R | R | R | R | R | R |
| sourceIdentityMode | R | R | R | R | R | R | R | R | R |
| sourceAssetReferences | C | C | C | C | C | C | C | C | C |
| generationRequestConfigurationIdentity | C | C | C | C | C | C | C | C | C |
| activeCategoryIdentity | O | R | C | R | R | R | R | R | R |
| primaryBucketType | O | R | C | R | R | R | R | R | R |
| primaryBucketIdentity | O | R | C | R | R | R | R | R | R |
| secondaryDiagnosticLabels | O | O | O | O | O | O | O | O | O |
| subsetIdentity | O | R | R | R | R | R | R | R | R |
| lineageGroupIdentity | O | R | R | R | R | R | R | R | R |
| sufficiencyAssessmentIdentity | O | R | C | R | R | R | R | R | R |
| completenessAssessmentIdentity | O | R | C | R | R | C | R | R | C |
| expectedFamilyAssignmentIdentity | P | R | C | R | R | R | R | R | R |
| predecessorCaseIdentity | P | P | P | P | C | O | R | O | O |
| replacementCaseIdentity | P | P | P | P | P | C | P | O | C |

| integrityReference | R | R | R | R | R | R | R | R | R |
| historyReference | R | R | R | R | R | R | R | R | R |

Conditional semantics:

```text
adjudication-required:
disputed values may be absent or represented as unresolved candidates;
no disputed value is treated as sealed.

sealed predecessorCaseIdentity:
required only for a successor revision.

excluded/replacement-required completeness:
required only when a completeness decision participated in exclusion.

source identity components:
governed by §14.3.
```

### 14.3 Source-identity-mode registry and cardinality

| Stable ID | Token | EN | RU | Definition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| c7.sourceidentitymode.001 | asset-backed | Asset-backed source identity | Идентичность источника на основе актива | Licensed or staged image source: at least one sourceAssetReference is required. |
| c7.sourceidentitymode.002 | synthetic-reference-backed | Reference-backed synthetic identity | Синтетическая идентичность с референсом | Synthetic generation with a reference source: sourceAssetReference and generation-request/configuration identity are both required. |
| c7.sourceidentitymode.003 | synthetic-text-only | Text-only synthetic identity | Синтетическая идентичность только по тексту | Text-only synthetic generation: sourceAssetReference is absent; generation-request/configuration identity is required. |

| Source mode | sourceAssetReference | generation-request/configuration identity | Validity |
|---|---|---|---|
| asset-backed licensed/staged | 1..N required | optional if no generation occurred | valid |
| synthetic-reference-backed | 1..N required | exactly 1 required | valid |
| synthetic-text-only | prohibited | exactly 1 required | valid |
| any mode with fabricated sourceAssetReference | invalid | invalid | BLOCKER |

Text-only synthetic generation never fabricates a sourceAssetReference.


## 15. Development and Held-Out Subsets

| Stable ID | Token | EN | RU | Definition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| c7.subset.001 | development | Development | Разработка | Corpus-development subset; no lineage group may cross to held-out. |
| c7.subset.002 | held-out | Held-out | Отложенная выборка | Sealed evaluation subset; ground truth is nondisclosed to evaluated providers. |

```text
Subset assignment occurs before sealing.
A sealed subset is immutable.
A lineage group is subset-atomic.
A post-seal transfer creates invalidation/supersession plus replacement
and recount; it never edits the sealed case in place.
```

### 16.4 Material-difference and qualitative predicate registry

| Stable ID | Token | EN | RU | Operational predicate |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| c7.qualifiercriterion.001 | materially-different-visible-evidence | Materially different visible evidence | Существенно отличающиеся видимые свидетельства | At least one independently annotatable Room, StructuralElement or Object evidence unit is added, removed, newly visible, no longer visible, or changes a sealed scenario/sufficiency/completeness decision. |
| c7.qualifiercriterion.002 | non-trivial-frame-exclusion | Non-trivial frame exclusion | Нетривиальное исключение кадром | At least one required entity/structure is clipped, or the excluded room portion is estimated at 5% or more of the visible room extent; uncertain estimates require adjudication. |
| c7.qualifiercriterion.003 | significant-occlusion | Significant occlusion | Значимое загораживание | At least 50% of an annotatable target is obscured, or a critical identification feature is hidden; uncertain cases require adjudication. |
| c7.qualifiercriterion.004 | sparse-visible-evidence | Sparse visible evidence | Редкие видимые свидетельства | No more than two independent design-relevant evidence units are visible after duplicate exclusion. |
| c7.qualifiercriterion.005 | incidental-object | Incidental object | Случайный объект | An Object occupying less than an estimated 5% of the visible frame and not used for category, sufficiency, scenario or completeness assignment; uncertainty requires adjudication. |
| c7.qualifiercriterion.006 | moderate-clutter-threshold | Moderate clutter threshold | Порог умеренной загромождённости | At least three additional Objects across at least two support surfaces or floor zones beyond category-essential objects, without causing insufficiency. |
| c7.qualifiercriterion.007 | typical-view-baseline | Typical view baseline | Базовый типовой ракурс | No visible high-, low- or oblique-angle cue materially changes evidence availability; uncertainty requires adjudication. |

If an operational threshold cannot be resolved from visible evidence,
`c7.adjudicationtrigger.007` is mandatory. No annotator may substitute
an ungoverned subjective interpretation.


## 17. Multi-Image RoomCase Handling

For `ImageAsset[1..6]`:

```text
one RoomCase identity;
one subset;
one primary bucket;
one sufficiency assessment;
one completeness assessment;
one expected family;
one population count.
```

Complete relationship × count matrix:

| Image relationship | Count effect | Assignment effect |
|---|---|---|
| 1–6 ImageAssets inside one RoomCase | exactly one count | all share case assignment |
| exact/near duplicate views | no additional count or evidence independence | preserve trace; deduplicate |
| materially different views in same RoomCase | still one count | aggregate evidence; preserve contradiction |
| views belonging to different rooms/material states | invalid RoomCase | reject/rebuild; never fuse |
| related but separate eligible RoomCases in same lineage/subset | each may count once | only under §16 eligibility |
| related RoomCases across subsets | invalid | lineage leakage |

No majority vote, averaging, automatic best-view selection or
contradiction deletion is allowed.


## 18. Semantic Sufficiency Architecture

### 18.1 Sufficiency-criterion registry

| Stable ID | Token | EN | RU | Definition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| c7.sufficiencycriterion.001 | room-identification-evidence | Room-identification evidence | Свидетельство идентификации комнаты | Evidence sufficient to resolve exactly one active Residential-34 category. |
| c7.sufficiencycriterion.002 | independent-additional-evidence-unit | Independent additional evidence unit | Независимая дополнительная единица свидетельства | A distinct StructuralElement or Object evidence unit not reused for category identification. |
| c7.sufficiencycriterion.003 | independence-requirement | Independence requirement | Требование независимости | The two sufficiency criteria rely on distinguishable visual loci/evidence units. |
| c7.sufficiencycriterion.004 | cross-view-aggregation | Cross-view aggregation | Агрегация между ракурсами | Evidence may be combined across ImageAssets inside the same RoomCase. |
| c7.sufficiencycriterion.005 | duplicate-evidence-exclusion | Duplicate-evidence exclusion | Исключение дублирующих свидетельств | The same underlying evidence counts once. |

### 18.2 Sufficiency-outcome registry

| Stable ID | Token | EN | RU | Definition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| c7.sufficiencyoutcome.001 | sufficient | Sufficient | Достаточно | Both required sufficiency criteria are satisfied without unresolved contradiction. |
| c7.sufficiencyoutcome.002 | insufficient | Insufficient | Недостаточно | At least one required sufficiency criterion is not satisfied after governed assessment. |

### 18.3 Closed evidence-combination matrix

| Room identification | Independent additional unit | Contradiction/ambiguity | Sufficiency outcome | Case handling |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| satisfied | satisfied | none | sufficient | continue classification |
| satisfied | not satisfied | none | insufficient | ordinary ineligible; special decision table |
| not satisfied | any | none | insufficient | special decision table |
| satisfied | satisfied | unresolved | unresolved process state | adjudication; no sealing |
| same cue reused | apparently satisfied | any | invalid | remove duplicate use and reassess |
| evidence duplicated across views | apparently satisfied | any | invalid independence | deduplicate and reassess |

Occlusion, blur, darkness, resolution and crop are not automatic
outcomes. They reduce the ability to satisfy one or both criteria.
Ambiguous effect requires adjudication.

Provider confidence, Contract-5 confidence and Contract-6
determinability never replace these criteria.


## 19. Expected Result-Family Assignment

### 19.1 Expected-family assignment-rule registry

| Stable ID | Token | EN | RU | Definition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| c7.expectedfamilyrule.001 | ordinary-scene-result | Ordinary SceneResult rule | Правило SceneResult для обычной ячейки | An ordinary-grid case must be sufficient, marker-negative and completeness=full; expected family is SceneResult. |
| c7.expectedfamilyrule.002 | low-information-by-sufficiency | Low-information expected-family rule | Правило ожидаемого семейства для малоинформативного случая | Expected family is SceneResult when sufficient, otherwise InsufficientEvidenceResult. |
| c7.expectedfamilyrule.003 | empty-by-sufficiency | Empty/near-empty expected-family rule | Правило ожидаемого семейства для пустой/почти пустой комнаты | Expected family is SceneResult when sufficient, otherwise InsufficientEvidenceResult. |
| c7.expectedfamilyrule.004 | meaningful-partial-scene-result | Meaningful partial SceneResult rule | Правило SceneResult для значимой частичной сцены | Sufficiency must be sufficient; at least one valid continuation marker exists; expected family is SceneResult and completeness=partial. |
| c7.expectedfamilyrule.005 | genuine-insufficient | Genuine insufficient-evidence rule | Правило подлинно недостаточных свидетельств | Expected family is InsufficientEvidenceResult. |

Expected-family assignment is:

```text
corpus-controlled;
evidence-based;
sealed before provider evaluation;
immutable after output;
one per RoomCase.
```

SemanticCases use only:

```text
SceneResult;
InsufficientEvidenceResult.
```

FailureResult and RejectedResult remain fixture outcomes outside the
semantic population.


## 20. Expected-Family Rules by Bucket

| Primary bucket | Sufficiency | Continuation marker | Expected family | Completeness | Validity |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ordinary cell | sufficient | absent and observability adequate | SceneResult | full | valid |
| ordinary cell | insufficient | any | — | — | invalid ordinary assignment; reclassify/replace |
| ordinary cell | sufficient | present | — | — | invalid ordinary assignment; meaningful-partial-scene |
| low-information | sufficient | absent | SceneResult | full | valid |
| low-information | insufficient | absent/irrelevant | InsufficientEvidenceResult | no SceneResult completeness value | valid |
| low-information | sufficient | present | SceneResult | partial | reclassify as meaningful-partial-scene |
| empty-or-near-empty | sufficient | absent | SceneResult | full | valid |
| empty-or-near-empty | insufficient | absent/irrelevant | InsufficientEvidenceResult | no SceneResult completeness value | valid |
| empty-or-near-empty | sufficient | present | SceneResult | partial | reclassify as meaningful-partial-scene |
| meaningful-partial-scene | sufficient | ≥1 valid marker | SceneResult | partial | valid |
| meaningful-partial-scene | insufficient | any | — | — | invalid; apply §12.2 |
| genuine-insufficient-evidence | insufficient | absent or present but not sufficient for partial SceneResult | InsufficientEvidenceResult | no SceneResult completeness value | valid |
| genuine-insufficient-evidence | sufficient | any | — | — | invalid; ordinary or meaningful-partial |

No low-information or empty/near-empty case remains without one sealed
expected family.


## 21. Completeness Architecture

Contract 7 is the sole owner of current sufficiency/completeness
annotation criteria and edge markers. Contract 9 and Contract 10 do not
own or complete this catalogue.

### 21.1 Completeness-criterion registry

| Stable ID | Token | EN | RU | Definition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| c7.completenesscriterion.001 | resolved-full | Resolved full criterion | Критерий подтверждённой полной сцены | Sufficiency is resolved; observability is adequate; no valid continuation marker exists; no marker conflict or unresolved boundary ambiguity remains. |
| c7.completenesscriterion.002 | resolved-partial | Resolved partial criterion | Критерий подтверждённой частичной сцены | Sufficiency is sufficient and at least one valid continuation marker exists without unresolved contradiction. |
| c7.completenesscriterion.003 | unresolved-not-sealable | Unresolved completeness criterion | Неразрешимый критерий полноты | Observability, marker validity or cross-view evidence is ambiguous or contradictory; no full/partial value may be sealed until adjudicated or replaced. |

### 21.2 Completeness-assessment-state registry

| Stable ID | Token | EN | RU | Definition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| c7.completenessassessmentstate.001 | unresolved | Unresolved | Не разрешено | No full/partial value may be sealed. |
| c7.completenessassessmentstate.002 | resolved-full | Resolved full | Подтверждено: full | Completeness is resolved to upstream value full under c7.completenesscriterion.001. |
| c7.completenessassessmentstate.003 | resolved-partial | Resolved partial | Подтверждено: partial | Completeness is resolved to upstream value partial under c7.completenesscriterion.002. |

### 21.3 Closed edge-continuation-marker registry

| Stable ID | Token | EN | RU | Positive predicate |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| c7.edgemarker.001 | structural-element-cut-by-frame | Structural element cut by frame | Структурный элемент обрезан кадром | A wall, ceiling plane, floor boundary, built-in structure or other same-room structural element visibly continues through the image boundary. |
| c7.edgemarker.002 | object-cut-by-frame | Object cut by frame | Объект обрезан кадром | A design-relevant Object visibly continues through the image boundary and the continuation is material to the scene. |
| c7.edgemarker.003 | same-room-geometry-continues-beyond-frame | Same-room geometry continues beyond frame | Геометрия той же комнаты продолжается за кадром | Visible floor, wall, ceiling or boundary geometry establishes continuation of the same physical room beyond the captured evidence boundary. |
| c7.edgemarker.004 | truncated-same-room-opening | Truncated same-room opening | Обрезанный проём той же комнаты | A truncated opening or open-plan boundary visibly continues the same room beyond the frame; a doorway to another room is explicitly excluded. |

### 21.4 Edge marker × completeness effect matrix

| Marker state | Sufficiency | Observability/conflict | Completeness state | Bucket effect |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| no valid marker | sufficient | adequate; no conflict | resolved-full | ordinary/low/empty according to §12.2 |
| ≥1 valid marker | sufficient | no unresolved conflict | resolved-partial | meaningful-partial-scene |
| no detected marker | sufficient | observability inadequate or ambiguous | unresolved | adjudication/replacement; never default full |
| marker evidence conflicts across views | sufficient | unresolved conflict | unresolved | adjudication/replacement |
| any marker state | insufficient | any | no SceneResult completeness value sealed | low/empty/genuine-insufficient by §12.2 |

A doorway to another room is not a marker. Only visibly continuous
same-room geometry or a truncated same-room/open-plan boundary qualifies.


## 22. Partial-Framing vs Meaningful-Partial-Scene Boundary

Closed rule:

```text
sealed completeness=partial
⇔
primary bucket = meaningful-partial-scene
AND
sufficiency=sufficient
AND
at least one valid continuation marker.
```

Ordinary `partial-room-framing` is valid only when:

```text
sufficiency=sufficient;
no valid continuation marker;
observability is adequate;
completeness=full.
```

Counterexamples:

| Case | Correct classification |
|---|---|
| frame excludes a corner, no marker, observability adequate | ordinary partial-room-framing / full |
| same-room wall/floor visibly continues through frame | meaningful-partial-scene / SceneResult / partial |
| doorway leads to another room | not an edge marker by itself |
| marker visible but room identification insufficient | not meaningful-partial; apply low/empty/genuine-insufficient |
| marker absent because image is too dark to assess | completeness unresolved; adjudication/replacement |


## 23. Relation and Applicability Compatibility

Contract 2 owns relation semantics. Contract 3 owns category
applicability. Contract 7 never changes either.

Complete imported disposition × Contract-7 behavior matrix:

| Imported Contract-3 disposition | Contract-7 behavior |
|---|---|
| applicable | relation may be annotated when Contract-2 evidence predicates are met |
| conditionally applicable | apply the exact Contract-3 condition; Contract 7 adds none |
| not applicable | absence is valid and never counts as missing/incomplete |
| prohibited/reserved | no positive relation assertion is permitted; Contract 7 cannot activate it |
| unresolved upstream conflict | block case sealing and escalate to c7.escalation.005 |

Scenario family is never derived solely from relation count, and
provider output never changes applicability.

| c7.replacementreason.001 | unresolved-ambiguity | Unresolved ambiguity | Неразрешённая неоднозначность | Remove from active count; create one new replacement case in the same required bucket/subset. |
| c7.replacementreason.002 | lineage-violation | Lineage violation | Нарушение происхождения | Remove violating count; repair split; create replacements until all minima recover. |

| c7.replacementreason.005 | source-identity-defect | Source identity defect | Дефект идентичности источника | Exclude defective case and replace after source identity is governed. |

Every non-trivial adjudication record includes:

```text
trigger;
visible evidence/basis;
candidate assignments;
disposition;
rationale;
authority reference;
history;
replacement link where applicable.
```

Project Owner is not the routine annotator/adjudicator.

A replacement case is a new `semanticCaseIdentity` beginning at draft.
It is connected through `c7.recordtype.006`; the excluded case itself
never transitions to draft.

| From | To | Disposition |

| draft | ready-for-sealing | invalid |
| draft | sealed | invalid |

| draft | superseded | invalid |

| draft | replacement-required | conditional — source/case defect requires replacement |

| annotation-complete | adjudication-required | conditional — §24 trigger |
| annotation-complete | ready-for-sealing | conditional — all validations pass |
| annotation-complete | sealed | invalid |
| annotation-complete | excluded | conditional — case cannot qualify |
| annotation-complete | superseded | invalid |

| annotation-complete | replacement-required | conditional — exclusion creates deficit |

| adjudication-required | replacement-required | conditional — exclude-and-replace or fail-to-adjudicate |
| ready-for-sealing | draft | invalid |
| ready-for-sealing | annotation-complete | conditional — pre-seal check reopens annotation |
| ready-for-sealing | adjudication-required | conditional — new trigger |
| ready-for-sealing | ready-for-sealing | allowed — state retention; no semantic mutation |
| ready-for-sealing | sealed | allowed — all sealing validations pass |
| ready-for-sealing | excluded | conditional — late defect |
| ready-for-sealing | superseded | invalid |
| ready-for-sealing | invalidated | conditional — upstream/identity defect |
| ready-for-sealing | replacement-required | conditional — late defect creates deficit |
| sealed | draft | invalid |
| sealed | annotation-complete | invalid |
| sealed | adjudication-required | invalid |
| sealed | ready-for-sealing | invalid |
| sealed | sealed | allowed — state retention; no semantic mutation |
| sealed | excluded | invalid |
| sealed | superseded | conditional — successor revision exists |
| sealed | invalidated | conditional — proven upstream/identity/factual defect |
| sealed | replacement-required | invalid |
| excluded | draft | invalid |
| excluded | annotation-complete | invalid |
| excluded | adjudication-required | invalid |
| excluded | ready-for-sealing | invalid |
| excluded | sealed | invalid |
| excluded | excluded | allowed — state retention; no semantic mutation |
| excluded | superseded | invalid |
| excluded | invalidated | conditional — exclusion record itself defective |
| excluded | replacement-required | allowed — replacement link/count repair pending |
| superseded | draft | invalid |
| superseded | annotation-complete | invalid |
| superseded | adjudication-required | invalid |
| superseded | ready-for-sealing | invalid |
| superseded | sealed | invalid |
| superseded | excluded | invalid |
| superseded | superseded | allowed — state retention; no semantic mutation |
| superseded | invalidated | invalid |

| invalidated | draft | invalid |
| invalidated | annotation-complete | invalid |
| invalidated | adjudication-required | invalid |
| invalidated | ready-for-sealing | invalid |

| replacement-required | sealed | invalid |

| replacement-required | superseded | invalid |
| replacement-required | invalidated | conditional — replacement request itself defective |
| replacement-required | replacement-required | allowed — state retention; no semantic mutation |

Sealed fields are immutable. A correction uses a successor revision.
Replacement is a separate new case linked to the excluded case; it is
not an excluded→draft transition of the same identity.


## 26. Metric and Denominator Boundary

ETAP Rev16 remains the sole owner of metrics, thresholds, denominators
and population counts.

Contract 7 owns only sealed case membership.

```text
IE sensitivity:
sealed expected family = InsufficientEvidenceResult.

IE specificity:
sealed expected family = SceneResult.

UNEXP-COMB:
uses sealed expected family;
produced output never changes membership.
```

No metric identity, blocking/diagnostic status, threshold,
zero-denominator rule or statistical method is changed here.


## 27. Contract-8, Contract-10 and Contract-11 Boundaries

```text
Contract 8:
NOT AUTHORIZED / NOT OPENED.
No unseen-claim codes, claim records or claim-level scoring.

Contract 9:
fixture subtype owner; not a completeness owner.

Contract 10:
NOT AUTHORIZED / NOT OPENED.
No JSON names, wire objects, schemas, nesting or serialization order.

Contract 11:
NOT AUTHORIZED / NOT OPENED.
No aggregation, uncertainty aggregation, score stability or metric
recombination.
```

Contract 7 exposes only future-consumer facts: case identity, active
category, primary bucket, subset, lineage, sufficiency, completeness,
expected family and evidence references.

## 29. Normative Rules

| Rule ID | Token | EN label | RU label | EN definition | RU definition | Validation mapping |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| c7.rule.001 | scenario-positive-predicate | Scenario Positive Predicate | Положительный предикат сценария | Every ordinary primary scenario assignment satisfies the selected scenario family's positive predicate. | Каждое основное назначение обычного сценария удовлетворяет положительному предикату выбранного семейства. | c7.validation.003 |
| c7.rule.002 | scenario-exclusion-predicate | Scenario Exclusion Predicate | Исключающий предикат сценария | No ordinary primary scenario assignment violates the selected scenario family's exclusion predicate. | Ни одно основное назначение обычного сценария не нарушает исключающий предикат выбранного семейства. | c7.validation.003 |
| c7.rule.003 | scenario-provider-independence | Scenario Provider Independence | Независимость сценария от провайдера | Scenario labels are sealed before mechanism evaluation and never inferred from provider output. | Сценарные метки запечатываются до оценки механизма и никогда не выводятся из ответа провайдера. | c7.validation.004 |
| c7.rule.004 | one-primary-bucket | One Primary Bucket | Один основной сегмент | Every active SemanticCase has exactly one primary bucket at and after annotation-complete. | Каждый активный SemanticCase имеет ровно один основной сегмент начиная с состояния annotation-complete. | c7.validation.005 |
| c7.rule.005 | secondary-label-non-counting | Secondary Label Non Counting | Вторичная метка не влияет на подсчёт | Secondary scenario labels never change primary counts, subset, expected family or completeness. | Вторичные сценарные метки не изменяют основной подсчёт, выборку, ожидаемое семейство или полноту. | c7.validation.006 |
| c7.rule.006 | multi-scenario-adjudication | Multi Scenario Adjudication | Разрешение пересечения сценариев | Multiple matching ordinary scenario predicates require adjudication; no invented precedence is applied. | Совпадение нескольких обычных сценарных предикатов требует разрешения неоднозначности; выдуманный приоритет запрещён. | c7.validation.003 |
| c7.rule.007 | full-ordinary-cell-registry | Full Ordinary Cell Registry | Полный реестр 204 ячеек | All 204 Residential-34 × six-scenario cells are materialized and applicable with dev=2 and held-out=3 minima. | Все 204 ячейки Residential-34 × шесть сценариев материализованы и имеют минимумы 2/3. | c7.validation.007 |
| c7.rule.008 | special-group-closed-set | Special Group Closed Set | Закрытый набор специальных групп | Exactly four primary special groups exist; no fifth group is activated. | Существуют ровно четыре основные специальные группы; пятая группа не активируется. | c7.validation.008 |
| c7.rule.009 | special-decision-order | Special Decision Order | Порядок специальной классификации | Special/ordinary classification follows the closed decision table in §12.2. | Классификация ordinary/special следует закрытой таблице решений §12.2. | c7.validation.009 |
| c7.rule.010 | ordinary-special-exclusivity | Ordinary Special Exclusivity | Взаимоисключение ordinary и special | A RoomCase counts in one ordinary cell or one special group, never both. | Один RoomCase учитывается либо в одной обычной ячейке, либо в одной специальной группе, но не одновременно. | c7.validation.005, c7.validation.009 |
| c7.rule.011 | unique-roomcase-count | Unique Roomcase Count | Уникальный подсчёт RoomCase | Primary assignment count equals unique RoomCase count, never ImageAsset count. | Число основных назначений равно числу уникальных RoomCase, а не ImageAsset. | c7.validation.010 |
| c7.rule.012 | active-residential-category | Active Residential Category | Активная жилая категория | Every case uses exactly one active c1.space.001–034 identity. | Каждый случай использует ровно одну активную идентичность c1.space.001–034. | c7.validation.001 |
| c7.rule.013 | bedroom-specialization-separation | Bedroom Specialization Separation | Раздельность специализаций спальни | children_room, guest_bedroom and primary_bedroom remain separate rows. | children_room, guest_bedroom и primary_bedroom остаются отдельными строками. | c7.validation.002 |
| c7.rule.014 | composite-profile-separation | Composite Profile Separation | Раздельность композитного профиля | kitchen_living_room remains one named composite-profile row. | kitchen_living_room остаётся одной строкой именованного композитного профиля. | c7.validation.002 |
| c7.rule.015 | semanticcase-identity-uniqueness | Semanticcase Identity Uniqueness | Уникальность SemanticCase | No two active case revisions share one semanticCaseIdentity. | Две активные ревизии случая не могут иметь одну semanticCaseIdentity. | c7.validation.011 |
| c7.rule.016 | identity-no-silent-rebinding | Identity No Silent Rebinding | Запрет скрытой перепривязки | Category, primary bucket, subset, lineage and expected family cannot be silently rebound. | Категория, основной сегмент, выборка, происхождение и ожидаемое семейство не перепривязываются скрыто. | c7.validation.012 |
| c7.rule.017 | conditional-cardinality | Conditional Cardinality | Кардинальность по жизненному циклу | SemanticCase component cardinality follows the lifecycle-state matrix in §14.2. | Кардинальность компонентов SemanticCase определяется матрицей состояний §14.2. | c7.validation.013 |
| c7.rule.018 | source-mode-cardinality | Source Mode Cardinality | Кардинальность identity источника | Source references follow the source-identity-mode matrix in §14.3. | Ссылки на источник определяются матрицей режима идентичности источника §14.3. | c7.validation.014 |
| c7.rule.019 | subset-exactly-one | Subset Exactly One | Ровно одна выборка | Every annotation-complete or later active case has exactly one subset. | Каждый активный случай начиная с annotation-complete имеет ровно одну выборку. | c7.validation.015 |
| c7.rule.020 | sealed-subset-immutability | Sealed Subset Immutability | Неизменность запечатанной выборки | Subset transfer after sealing requires invalidation/supersession and replacement. | Перенос запечатанного случая между выборками требует invalidation/supersession и замены. | c7.validation.015 |
| c7.rule.021 | lineage-same-subset | Lineage Same Subset | Одна выборка для lineage | Every governed lineage group belongs wholly to development or wholly to held-out. | Вся группа происхождения целиком относится либо к development, либо к held-out. | c7.validation.016 |
| c7.rule.022 | lineage-count-eligibility | Lineage Count Eligibility | Допустимость уникального подсчёта lineage | A derivative counts uniquely only when its lineage relation is eligible and material evidence differs. | Производный случай считается уникальным только при допустимом типе происхождения и существенном отличии свидетельств. | c7.validation.017 |
| c7.rule.023 | technical-transform-noncounting | Technical Transform Noncounting | Технические преобразования не считаются | Crop, resize, rotation, exact duplicate and near duplicate never inflate counts. | Crop, resize, rotation, exact duplicate и near duplicate не увеличивают число случаев. | c7.validation.010 |
| c7.rule.024 | material-difference-predicate | Material Difference Predicate | Предикат существенного отличия | Material difference is determined only by c7.qualifiercriterion.001. | Существенное отличие определяется только c7.qualifiercriterion.001. | c7.validation.017 |
| c7.rule.025 | multi-image-shared-assignment | Multi Image Shared Assignment | Общее назначение для multi-image | All ImageAssets in one RoomCase share subset, primary bucket and one expected-family assignment. | Все ImageAsset одного RoomCase имеют одну выборку, один основной сегмент и одно ожидаемое семейство. | c7.validation.018 |
| c7.rule.026 | multi-image-single-count | Multi Image Single Count | Один count для multi-image | One RoomCase counts once regardless of its 1–6 ImageAssets. | Один RoomCase учитывается один раз независимо от 1–6 ImageAsset. | c7.validation.010 |
| c7.rule.027 | cross-view-consolidation | Cross View Consolidation | Консолидация cross-view evidence | Sufficiency and completeness use consolidated in-scope evidence with contradictions preserved. | Достаточность и полнота используют объединённые допустимые свидетельства с сохранением противоречий. | c7.validation.019 |
| c7.rule.028 | no-view-majority | No View Majority | Запрет голосования по ракурсам | No majority vote, averaging or automatic best-view selection determines labels. | Голосование по большинству, усреднение и автоматический выбор лучшего ракурса запрещены. | c7.validation.019 |
| c7.rule.029 | sufficiency-two-criterion | Sufficiency Two Criterion | Двухкритериальная sufficiency | SceneResult eligibility requires room-identification evidence plus one independent additional evidence unit. | Допуск SceneResult требует идентификации комнаты и одной независимой дополнительной единицы свидетельства. | c7.validation.020 |
| c7.rule.030 | sufficiency-cue-independence | Sufficiency Cue Independence | Независимость признаков sufficiency | One visual cue cannot satisfy both sufficiency criteria. | Один визуальный признак не может удовлетворять двум критериям достаточности. | c7.validation.021 |
| c7.rule.031 | duplicate-evidence-exclusion | Duplicate Evidence Exclusion | Исключение duplicate evidence | Duplicate evidence across or within views counts once. | Дублирующее свидетельство внутри или между ракурсами учитывается один раз. | c7.validation.021 |
| c7.rule.032 | confidence-not-sufficiency | Confidence Not Sufficiency | Уверенность не заменяет sufficiency | Provider confidence and Contract-5/6 states never substitute for Contract-7 sufficiency. | Уверенность провайдера и состояния Contract 5/6 не заменяют достаточность Contract 7. | c7.validation.022 |
| c7.rule.033 | sufficiency-ambiguity-adjudication | Sufficiency Ambiguity Adjudication | Неоднозначность sufficiency требует adjudication | Category, evidence or independence ambiguity blocks sealing until adjudicated or replaced. | Неоднозначность категории, свидетельств или независимости блокирует запечатывание до разрешения или замены. | c7.validation.023 |
| c7.rule.034 | expected-family-pre-output | Expected Family Pre Output | Expected family до вывода | Expected family is assigned and sealed before mechanism evaluation. | Ожидаемое семейство назначается и запечатывается до оценки механизма. | c7.validation.024 |
| c7.rule.035 | expected-family-output-immutability | Expected Family Output Immutability | Неизменность expected family после вывода | Produced output cannot alter the sealed expected family. | Сформированный результат не может изменить запечатанное ожидаемое семейство. | c7.validation.024 |
| c7.rule.036 | semantic-result-family-closed-set | Semantic Result Family Closed Set | Закрытый набор semantic result family | SemanticCases use only SceneResult or InsufficientEvidenceResult; FailureResult/RejectedResult remain fixture outcomes. | SemanticCase использует только SceneResult или InsufficientEvidenceResult; FailureResult/RejectedResult относятся к fixtures. | c7.validation.025 |
| c7.rule.037 | ordinary-scene-result | Ordinary Scene Result | SceneResult для ordinary | An ordinary case is sufficient, marker-negative, completeness=full and SceneResult-expected. | Обычный случай достаточен, не имеет маркера продолжения, имеет completeness=full и ожидает SceneResult. | c7.validation.026 |
| c7.rule.038 | low-information-expected-family | Low Information Expected Family | Expected family для low-information | Low-information expected family follows sufficiency. | Ожидаемое семейство малоинформативного случая определяется достаточностью. | c7.validation.026 |
| c7.rule.039 | empty-expected-family | Empty Expected Family | Expected family для empty | Empty/near-empty expected family follows sufficiency. | Ожидаемое семейство пустой/почти пустой комнаты определяется достаточностью. | c7.validation.026 |
| c7.rule.040 | meaningful-partial-required-combination | Meaningful Partial Required Combination | Условия meaningful-partial | Meaningful-partial-scene requires sufficient evidence plus at least one valid continuation marker. | Meaningful-partial-scene требует достаточности и хотя бы одного валидного маркера продолжения. | c7.validation.026 |
| c7.rule.041 | genuine-insufficient-fallback | Genuine Insufficient Fallback | Fallback genuine-insufficient | Genuine-insufficient-evidence is used only when insufficient and neither low-information nor empty/near-empty predicates apply. | Genuine-insufficient-evidence применяется только при недостаточности и отсутствии предикатов low-information и empty-or-near-empty. | c7.validation.026 |
| c7.rule.042 | completeness-owned-here | Completeness Owned Here | Полнота принадлежит Contract 7 | All completeness criteria and edge markers are fully owned and closed in Contract 7. | Все критерии полноты и edge markers полностью принадлежат Contract 7. | c7.validation.027 |
| c7.rule.043 | full-requires-observability | Full Requires Observability | Full требует наблюдаемости | Completeness=full requires adequate observability, no valid marker and no unresolved marker conflict. | Completeness=full требует достаточной наблюдаемости, отсутствия валидного маркера и неразрешённого конфликта. | c7.validation.028 |
| c7.rule.044 | partial-requires-marker-and-sufficiency | Partial Requires Marker And Sufficiency | Partial требует marker и sufficiency | Completeness=partial requires sufficient evidence and at least one valid continuation marker. | Completeness=partial требует достаточности и минимум одного валидного маркера продолжения. | c7.validation.029 |
| c7.rule.045 | completeness-unresolved-unsealable | Completeness Unresolved Unsealable | Unresolved completeness не запечатывается | Ambiguous or contradictory completeness remains unresolved and unsealable; absence of a detected marker alone never proves full. | Неоднозначная или противоречивая полнота остаётся unresolved и не запечатывается; отсутствие найденного маркера само по себе не доказывает full. | c7.validation.028 |
| c7.rule.046 | doorway-marker-narrowness | Doorway Marker Narrowness | Узкое определение doorway marker | A doorway to another room is not a continuation marker; only a truncated same-room opening qualifies. | Дверь в другую комнату не является маркером продолжения; подходит только обрезанный проём той же комнаты. | c7.validation.030 |
| c7.rule.047 | partial-implies-special | Partial Implies Special | Partial означает meaningful-partial | Every sealed partial SemanticCase belongs to meaningful-partial-scene. | Каждый запечатанный partial SemanticCase относится к meaningful-partial-scene. | c7.validation.031 |
| c7.rule.048 | partial-framing-full-only | Partial Framing Full Only | Partial-room-framing только full | An ordinary partial-room-framing case is valid only with completeness=full and no valid continuation marker. | Обычный partial-room-framing допустим только с completeness=full и без валидного маркера. | c7.validation.031 |
| c7.rule.049 | relation-ownership | Relation Ownership | Владение relation semantics | Contract 2/3 relation semantics and applicability remain unchanged. | Семантика и применимость отношений Contract 2/3 не изменяются. | c7.validation.032 |
| c7.rule.050 | inapplicable-relation-not-missing | Inapplicable Relation Not Missing | Неприменимое relation не является пропуском | Absence of a Contract-3-inapplicable relation never creates Contract-7 incompleteness. | Отсутствие неприменимого по Contract 3 отношения не создаёт неполноту Contract 7. | c7.validation.032 |
| c7.rule.051 | adjudication-rationale | Adjudication Rationale | Полная rationale adjudication | Every non-trivial adjudication disposition records rationale, evidence and authority reference. | Каждое нетривиальное решение adjudication содержит обоснование, свидетельства и ссылку на полномочие. | c7.validation.033 |
| c7.rule.052 | project-owner-not-routine-adjudicator | Project Owner Not Routine Adjudicator | Owner не штатный adjudicator | Project Owner is not the routine case adjudicator. | Project Owner не назначается штатным разрешающим аннотационные неоднозначности. | c7.validation.033 |
| c7.rule.053 | unresolved-case-exclusion | Unresolved Case Exclusion | Unresolved case исключается | Fail-to-adjudicate cases are not sealed and require exclusion/replacement. | Случай fail-to-adjudicate не запечатывается и требует исключения/замены. | c7.validation.034 |
| c7.rule.054 | replacement-count-repair | Replacement Count Repair | Replacement восстанавливает count | Every exclusion repairs the exact deficient bucket/subset minimum. | Каждое исключение восстанавливает точный минимум дефицитного сегмента и выборки. | c7.validation.034 |
| c7.rule.055 | lifecycle-closed-matrix | Lifecycle Closed Matrix | Закрытая lifecycle matrix | Every lifecycle state-to-state pair has one explicit disposition. | Каждая пара состояний жизненного цикла имеет явную диспозицию. | c7.validation.035 |
| c7.rule.056 | sealed-property-immutability | Sealed Property Immutability | Неизменность sealed fields | All sealed semantic assignments are immutable. | Все запечатанные семантические назначения неизменяемы. | c7.validation.012 |
| c7.rule.057 | successor-revision-only | Successor Revision Only | Исправление только successor revision | Post-seal correction creates a successor case revision with predecessor linkage. | Исправление после запечатывания создаёт ревизию-преемника со ссылкой на предшественника. | c7.validation.012 |
| c7.rule.058 | replacement-is-new-case | Replacement Is New Case | Replacement — новый case | A replacement begins as a new draft case identity; excluded→draft is not an in-place transition. | Замена начинается как новый draft case; excluded→draft не является переходом того же случая. | c7.validation.035 |
| c7.rule.059 | etap-metrics-unchanged | Etap Metrics Unchanged | ETAP не изменяется | Contract 7 does not alter ETAP metrics, thresholds, denominators or population totals. | Contract 7 не меняет метрики, пороги, знаменатели и численность ETAP. | c7.validation.036 |
| c7.rule.060 | population-membership-presealed | Population Membership Presealed | Population membership по sealed assignment | Metric populations are determined from sealed expected family and bucket, never produced output. | Принадлежность к метрике определяется запечатанным ожидаемым семейством и сегментом, а не фактическим выводом. | c7.validation.036 |
| c7.rule.061 | contract8-boundary | Contract8 Boundary | Граница Contract 8 | No unseen-claim vocabulary or claim-level scoring is defined. | Словарь unseen claims и claim-level scoring не определяются. | c7.validation.037 |
| c7.rule.062 | contract10-boundary | Contract10 Boundary | Граница Contract 10 | No final serialization, wire names or schemas are fixed. | Финальная сериализация, wire names и схемы не фиксируются. | c7.validation.037 |
| c7.rule.063 | contract11-boundary | Contract11 Boundary | Граница Contract 11 | No aggregation or score-stability architecture is defined. | Архитектура агрегации и устойчивости score не определяется. | c7.validation.037 |
| c7.rule.064 | real-user-photo-prohibition | Real User Photo Prohibition | Запрет реальных пользовательских фото | Only licensed, synthetic and staged sources are permitted; real user photos are prohibited. | Разрешены только licensed, synthetic и staged источники; реальные пользовательские фото запрещены. | c7.validation.038 |
| c7.rule.065 | commercial-prohibition | Commercial Prohibition | Запрет commercial scope | Commercial property is excluded from the active Contract-7 population. | Коммерческая недвижимость исключена из активной популяции Contract 7. | c7.validation.038 |
| c7.rule.066 | learning-ready-not-active | Learning Ready Not Active | Learning-ready, not learning-active | Controlled Learning remains learning-ready and not learning-active. | Controlled Learning остаётся learning-ready и not learning-active. | c7.validation.039 |
| c7.rule.067 | localization-completeness | Localization Completeness | Полная EN/RU localization | Every exposed Contract-7 stable target has EN/RU label and EN/RU definition. | Каждая открытая stable identity Contract 7 имеет EN/RU label и EN/RU definition. | c7.validation.040 |
| c7.rule.068 | qualifier-governance | Qualifier Governance | Управляемые качественные пороги | Every qualitative threshold uses a c7.qualifiercriterion.* predicate or adjudication. | Каждый качественный порог использует c7.qualifiercriterion.* либо обязательное adjudication. | c7.validation.041 |
| c7.rule.069 | implementation-compatibility-only | Implementation Compatibility Only | Implementation только для compatibility | Implementation source inspection establishes compatibility only and does not redefine normative semantics. | Проверка implementation sources устанавливает только совместимость и не переопределяет норму. | c7.validation.042 |
| c7.rule.070 | corpus-not-authorized | Corpus Not Authorized | Corpus activity не авторизована | This contract specifies corpus semantics but does not authorize corpus creation or annotation execution. | Контракт задаёт семантику корпуса, но не разрешает создание корпуса или выполнение аннотации. | c7.validation.043 |
| c7.rule.071 | contract8-not-opened | Contract8 Not Opened | Contract 8 не открыт | Contract 8 and Contracts 8–10 remain not authorized and not opened. | Contract 8 и Contracts 8–10 остаются неавторизованными и неоткрытыми. | c7.validation.037 |
| c7.rule.072 | repository-unchanged | Repository Unchanged | Repository не изменён | Preparation/correction does not modify the repository or claim persistence. | Подготовка/коррекция не изменяет репозиторий и не заявляет persistence. | c7.validation.044 |

Every rule maps to at least one validation. No narrative-only coverage
claim is used.


## 30. Validation Architecture

### 30.1 Validation-scope registry

| Stable ID | Token | EN | RU | Definition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| c7.validationscope.001 | contract-definition | Contract definition | Определение контракта | Checks normative document completeness and ownership. |
| c7.validationscope.002 | registry-lint | Registry lint | Проверка реестра | Checks stable IDs, counts, duplicates and references. |
| c7.validationscope.003 | case-instance | Case instance | Экземпляр случая | Checks one SemanticCase instance. |
| c7.validationscope.004 | population-and-lineage | Population and lineage | Популяция и происхождение | Checks counts, subsets and lineage leakage. |
| c7.validationscope.005 | sufficiency-and-completeness | Sufficiency and completeness | Достаточность и полнота | Checks evidence and completeness decisions. |
| c7.validationscope.006 | sealing-and-lifecycle | Sealing and lifecycle | Запечатывание и жизненный цикл | Checks transitions, immutability and successor history. |
| c7.validationscope.007 | cross-cutting-ownership | Cross-cutting ownership | Сквозное владение | Checks upstream/downstream and authorization boundaries. |
| c7.validationscope.008 | localization | Localization | Локализация | Checks complete EN/RU label and definition coverage. |

### 30.2 Validation-phase registry

| Stable ID | Token | EN | RU | Definition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| c7.validationphase.001 | authoring-lint | Authoring lint | Проверка при составлении | Runs while drafting/reviewing the contract. |
| c7.validationphase.002 | pre-annotation | Pre-annotation | До аннотирования | Runs before case annotation begins. |
| c7.validationphase.003 | annotation-and-assessment | Annotation and assessment | Аннотирование и оценка | Runs while assigning case semantics. |
| c7.validationphase.004 | adjudication | Adjudication | Разрешение неоднозначности | Runs during governed ambiguity resolution. |
| c7.validationphase.005 | sealing | Sealing | Запечатывание | Runs before sealing a case or population. |
| c7.validationphase.006 | post-sealing | Post-sealing | После запечатывания | Runs against immutable sealed history. |

### 30.3 Validation registry

| Validation ID | Token | Condition | Scope | Phase | Severity | Required handling | Primary failure | Rules |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| c7.validation.001 | missing-or-invalid-category | Active category missing, duplicated or outside c1.space.001–034 | case-instance | pre-annotation | MAJOR | Block annotation-complete; repair category assignment. | c7.failure.001 | c7.rule.012 |
| c7.validation.002 | specialization-or-composite-collapse | Bedroom specialization or kitchen_living_room collapsed into another row | contract-definition | authoring-lint | BLOCKER | Reject candidate definition. | c7.failure.002 | c7.rule.013, c7.rule.014 |
| c7.validation.003 | scenario-predicate-failure | Primary ordinary scenario fails its positive/exclusion predicates | case-instance | annotation-and-assessment | MAJOR | Route to adjudication or reassign before sealing. | c7.failure.003 | c7.rule.001, c7.rule.002, c7.rule.006 |
| c7.validation.004 | provider-driven-scenario | Scenario assignment derived from provider output | cross-cutting-ownership | sealing | BLOCKER | Discard assignment; restore pre-output corpus-controlled label. | c7.failure.004 | c7.rule.003 |
| c7.validation.005 | missing-or-multiple-primary-bucket | Primary bucket missing or more than one primary bucket assigned | case-instance | annotation-and-assessment | MAJOR | Block sealing; adjudicate or replace. | c7.failure.005 | c7.rule.004, c7.rule.010 |
| c7.validation.006 | secondary-label-count-mutation | Secondary label changes count, subset, expected family or completeness | population-and-lineage | sealing | MAJOR | Recount from primary assignments only; repair case. | c7.failure.006 | c7.rule.005 |
| c7.validation.007 | ordinary-cell-registry-incomplete | Any of 204 ordinary cells missing, duplicated or not applicable | registry-lint | authoring-lint | BLOCKER | Reject document until all 204 rows are explicit. | c7.failure.007 | c7.rule.007 |
| c7.validation.008 | special-group-registry-invalid | Special-group set missing/duplicated or contains fifth active group | registry-lint | authoring-lint | BLOCKER | Reject document; restore closed four-group registry. | c7.failure.008 | c7.rule.008 |
| c7.validation.009 | special-classification-overlap | Decision table yields multiple primary special groups or ordinary+special | case-instance | annotation-and-assessment | MAJOR | Apply §12.2 order; adjudicate unresolved predicates. | c7.failure.009 | c7.rule.009, c7.rule.010 |
| c7.validation.010 | roomcase-count-inflation | ImageAssets, duplicates or ineligible derivatives inflate counts | population-and-lineage | sealing | BLOCKER | Remove inflated counts and create governed replacements. | c7.failure.010 | c7.rule.011, c7.rule.023, c7.rule.026 |
| c7.validation.011 | duplicate-semanticcase-identity | Two active revisions share one semanticCaseIdentity | case-instance | pre-annotation | BLOCKER | Invalidate duplicate identity; assign governed successor identity. | c7.failure.011 | c7.rule.015 |
| c7.validation.012 | silent-identity-rebinding | Category/bucket/subset/lineage/expected family changed without successor | sealing-and-lifecycle | post-sealing | BLOCKER | Invalidate mutation; restore predecessor and create successor. | c7.failure.012 | c7.rule.016, c7.rule.056, c7.rule.057 |
| c7.validation.013 | lifecycle-cardinality-violation | Semantic component presence violates §14.2 state matrix | case-instance | sealing | MAJOR | Block transition; supply or clear fields per state matrix. | c7.failure.013 | c7.rule.017 |
| c7.validation.014 | source-identity-cardinality-violation | Source references violate §14.3 source-mode matrix | case-instance | pre-annotation | BLOCKER | Block case; repair source identity without fabrication. | c7.failure.014 | c7.rule.018 |
| c7.validation.015 | subset-missing-or-transfer | Subset missing or sealed subset transferred in place | population-and-lineage | post-sealing | BLOCKER | Invalidate/supersede; replace and recount. | c7.failure.015 | c7.rule.019, c7.rule.020 |
| c7.validation.016 | cross-subset-lineage | One lineage group spans development and held-out | population-and-lineage | sealing | BLOCKER | Remove leakage; keep lineage atomic; replace deficits. | c7.failure.016 | c7.rule.021 |
| c7.validation.017 | lineage-eligibility-violation | Derivative counted uniquely without eligible relation/material difference | population-and-lineage | sealing | MAJOR | Remove count; adjudicate material difference or replace. | c7.failure.017 | c7.rule.022, c7.rule.024 |
| c7.validation.018 | multi-image-assignment-split | Views in one RoomCase have different subset/bucket/expected family | population-and-lineage | annotation-and-assessment | BLOCKER | Merge to one RoomCase assignment or reject mixed set. | c7.failure.018 | c7.rule.025 |
| c7.validation.019 | cross-view-shortcut | Majority vote, averaging, best-view shortcut or contradiction deletion used | sufficiency-and-completeness | annotation-and-assessment | MAJOR | Re-derive from consolidated evidence with contradiction preserved. | c7.failure.019 | c7.rule.027, c7.rule.028 |

### 30.4 Bidirectional coverage invariant

```text
Every c7.rule.* appears in §30.3 Rules.
Every c7.validation.* maps to at least one c7.rule.*.
Every validation has exactly one primary failure.
Every failure maps to exactly one primary escalation.
```

| c7.escalation.013 | unauthorized-learning-activation | Unauthorized learning activation | Неавторизованная активация обучения | Stop automatic mutation, feedback use or training; preserve learning-ready/not-active boundary. |

## 33. Controlled Learning Compatibility

```text
LEARNING-READY
NOT LEARNING-ACTIVE
```

Allowed compatibility hooks:

```text
contract/scenario/population/lineage/sufficiency/completeness/
adjudication versions;
trace;
evidence;
provenance;
integrity;
immutable history;
evaluation hooks;
rollback compatibility.
```

Prohibited:

```text
automatic scenario reclassification;
automatic expected-family mutation;
automatic sufficiency/completeness rule changes;
feedback-driven population mutation;
training from user data;
learning analytics;
personalization as global learning signal;
self-modifying production behavior.
```


## 34. EN/RU Localization Registry

English is canonical. Russian is a complete derived locale. Stable IDs
are language-neutral. English fallback applies only if a future
translation is absent; no target in this candidate is missing Russian.

| Loc ID | Target | EN label | RU label | EN definition | RU definition |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| c7.loc.001 | c7.semanticmodelversion.001 | Candidate A Rev1 CC1 semantic model | Семантическая модель Candidate A Rev1 CC1 | Contract-7 semantic model after Correction Cycle 1; external byte identity is computed at freeze. | Семантическая модель Contract 7 после Correction Cycle 1; внешняя byte identity вычисляется при freeze. |
| c7.loc.002 | c7.recordtype.001 | SemanticCase record | Запись SemanticCase | Carries the case-level semantic assignment and lifecycle identity. | Содержит семантическое назначение уровня случая и идентичность жизненного цикла. |

| c7.loc.005 | c7.recordtype.004 | Completeness-assessment record | Запись оценки полноты | Carries completeness state, marker evidence and adjudication status. | Содержит состояние полноты, marker evidence и статус adjudication. |
| c7.loc.006 | c7.recordtype.005 | Adjudication record | Запись разрешения неоднозначности | Carries trigger, disposition, rationale, evidence and authority. | Содержит trigger, disposition, rationale, evidence и authority reference. |
| c7.loc.007 | c7.recordtype.006 | Replacement-link record | Запись связи замены | Connects an excluded case to a new replacement case identity. | Связывает исключённый случай с новой идентичностью replacement case. |

| c7.loc.010 | c7.primarybuckettype.001 | Ordinary | Обычный | Primary bucket is one of 204 category×scenario cells. | Основной сегмент — одна из 204 ячеек category×scenario. |

| c7.loc.012 | c7.scenariofamily.001 | Normal readable | Обычная, легко читаемая сцена | RoomCase is clearly legible and none of the other five scenario predicates dominates. | RoomCase отчётливо читаем; ни один из пяти остальных сценарных предикатов не является доминирующим. |
| c7.loc.013 | c7.scenariofamily.002 | Moderate clutter | Умеренная загромождённость | At least three additional visible Objects distributed across at least two support surfaces or floor zones are present beyond category-essential objects, while sufficiency remains resolvable. | Помимо объектов, необходимых для идентификации категории, видны не менее трёх дополнительных объектов минимум на двух опорных поверхностях или участках пола; достаточность остаётся разрешимой. |
| c7.loc.014 | c7.scenariofamily.003 | Partial occlusion | Частичное загораживание | An identifiable foreground object obscures at least half of an annotatable target or hides a category-, relation-, sufficiency- or completeness-critical feature; ambiguous cases require adjudication. | Идентифицируемый объект переднего плана закрывает не менее половины аннотируемой цели либо скрывает признак, критичный для категории, отношения, достаточности или полноты; неоднозначные случаи требуют разрешения. |
| c7.loc.015 | c7.scenariofamily.004 | Partial-room framing | Частичный кадр комнаты | The frame excludes a non-trivial portion of the room, but no valid continuation marker is sealed; therefore the case can remain ordinary only with completeness=full. | Кадр исключает нетривиальную часть комнаты, но валидный маркер продолжения не запечатан; поэтому случай может оставаться обычным только при completeness=full. |
| c7.loc.016 | c7.scenariofamily.005 | Lighting variation | Вариация освещения | Visible lighting materially alters scene legibility or appearance without making the RoomCase insufficient; examples include strong backlight, low ambient light, colored light or high contrast. | Видимое освещение существенно меняет читаемость или внешний вид сцены, но не делает RoomCase недостаточным; примеры: сильный контровой свет, слабое общее освещение, цветной свет или высокий контраст. |
| c7.loc.017 | c7.scenariofamily.006 | Camera-angle variation | Вариация ракурса камеры | The visible viewpoint is materially high, low or oblique and changes visible evidence compared with a typical staging view, while sufficiency remains resolvable. | Видимая точка съёмки существенно высокая, низкая или косая и меняет доступные свидетельства относительно типового постановочного ракурса, при этом достаточность остаётся разрешимой. |
| c7.loc.018 | c7.specialgroup.001 | Low-information | Малоинформативный | The case is not empty/near-empty; it contains no more than two independent design-relevant evidence units and may be sufficient or insufficient. | Случай не является пустым/почти пустым; он содержит не более двух независимых значимых единиц свидетельства и может быть достаточным или недостаточным. |
| c7.loc.019 | c7.specialgroup.002 | Empty or near-empty | Пустая или почти пустая комната | The room contains zero design-relevant Objects or one incidental Object that does not contribute to category, sufficiency, scenario or completeness assignment. | Комната содержит ноль значимых объектов либо один случайный объект, не влияющий на категорию, достаточность, сценарий или полноту. |
| c7.loc.020 | c7.specialgroup.003 | Meaningful partial-scene | Значимая частичная сцена | The case is sufficient and at least one valid continuation marker is sealed; expected family is SceneResult and completeness is partial. | Случай достаточен, и запечатан минимум один валидный маркер продолжения; ожидаемое семейство — SceneResult, полнота — partial. |
| c7.loc.021 | c7.specialgroup.004 | Genuine insufficient-evidence | Подлинно недостаточные свидетельства | The case is insufficient and satisfies neither empty/near-empty nor low-information content-shape predicates after adjudication. | Случай недостаточен и после разрешения неоднозначности не удовлетворяет предикатам пустой/почти пустой или малоинформативной сцены. |
